### Page 1

ab 18 September 2026
Global Research
Nanya Technology
Pricing reset driving upside in 3Q
3Q26 tracking well ahead of consensus
Nanya Tech's July ’26 revs were up 49% MoM and August 2%. Combined, July+August
is equivalent to 80% of VA consensus 3Q revenue of NT$111bn. We understand this
comes from both DRAM bit shipments and ASP upside. We now forecast QoQ bits up
5% vs -8% prior. More noticeably, we now forecast DRAM ASPs up 50% QoQ (vs.
+32% prior). We believe this comes from some 6-month to 1-year contracts which re-

set last July, leading to well above average QoQ pricing momentum. Also, with niche
DRAM exposure and less concentration around large clients, Nanya’s DRAM ASPs are
edging higher than its much larger peers as the upcycle continues. Looking into 4Q26,
we forecast ASPs to increase 10% QoQ. With that, gross margin should improve in
3Q26 to 86.1% from 79.5% in 2Q, and remain at elevated levels throughout 2027.
Reiterate Buy.
Not fulfilling customers’ demand until the next fab ramps

Nanya sees 50-60% fulfillment rate for demand currently. Several large customers are
not getting any allocation. Long term agreements cover >60% of bits already. Its new
fab will start equipment moving-in in 1Q27, with initial output in 3Q27. Initial capacity
will reach 30k wpm by 2028, with total theoretical capacity of 45-50k wpm. Nanya is
also constructing an EUV litho building with equipment moving in in 1Q28. The goal is
to secure 2 tools with space up to 5. Up to its 1d nm (which is 1a nm equivalent), Nanya

does not require EUV litho.
Increasing forecasts
We increase 3Q26E revs/EPS estimates to NT$133bn/NT$26.44 (by 30%/40%),
20%/37% above Visible Alpha consensus of NT$110bn/NT$19.26. We also increase
2026E/2027E/2028E EPS forecasts by 23%/12%/18% respectively on higher
DRAM ASP assumptions (9%/9%/4% higher per Gb vs prior).
Valuation: PT to NT$640 from NT$555; maintain Buy
We value Nanya Tech shares at 3.16x NTM P/BV (was 3.01x) based on our LT
ROE estimate of 26.5% (was 25.5%) and CoE of 8.4% (was 8.5%).

### Page 2

Nanya Technology 18 September 2026 ab 2
Nanya Technology UBS ResearchNanya Tech (2408.TW)
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will DRAM undersupply continue into 2028?
Yes in our view. Agentic AI is boosting further memory demand on multiple fronts beyond HBM,
including DDR5/LPDDR5 for conventional servers and CPU head nodes in AI servers. We now forecast
an acceleration of memory demand growth into 2027 with DRAM bit end-consumption growing

40% YoY vs 21% in 2026. As almost all of the incremental wafer capacity addition for DRAM is
going to HBM, supply is not catching up with demand. The main risk remains affordability by
customers as memory industry revenues are expected to reach US$1.64tn in 2027E.
Q: Can NTC grow as a niche DRAM player?
Yes. 2026 and 2027 are likely to be years of more constrained growth. Subsequently though, we
think its process tech migration to 1c nm and 1d nm will likely lead to a 30% increase in bits per wafer

for each process tech migration (at comparable yields). NTC is also slightly pulling in its new fab ramp,
with equipment expected to move in 1Q27. We also see a tail of DDR3 and DDR4 products NTC can
address for a while.
Nanya Tech shares trade at 2.59x NTM P/BV, above +1SD of its 10-year average. That said, we believe
the strength of the unfolding DRAM cycle as well as the tail of legacy products that Nanya can
address as a niche player should continue to provide support for the stock.

Our industry checks suggest HBM demand continues to result in capacity allocation away from DDR,
which may moderate possible cyclical corrections, supporting upside to DDR contract pricing for
longer.
At 2.59x NTM P/BV, we believe Nanya Tech shares are discounting a LT average ROE of 21.7% vs our
LT estimate of 26.5%.

Company Description
Nanya Tech is a pure DRAM company based in Taiwan, focusing on legacy products for
consumers, broad-based applications and is now growing in servers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

ab 18 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Wiwynn
Reaffirm the strong   growth through 2027
New platforms support AI strength into 2027 while general server slower
before next ramp in 2027
We maintain our Buy on Wiwynn with a price target of $ 2,470  and also in the
UBS regional most preferred tech list. Amid the market noise, we stay positive on the
company's growth, with catalysts from liquid cooled ASIC servers in initial production
and ramping up further through 2027 and Helios racks on track as a new project

commencing from late 2026E through 2027. We do see general server cooling into Sep
and Q426 to offset the ASIC server ramp before picking up from Q127 as customers
transition to the next-generation Clearwater Forest/Venice platforms.
August sales ahead supported by general server growth
Wiwynn August sales of NT$144.3bn, +22.6% MoM, 50.4% YoY tracked ahead at
74%/73% UBSe/Street of +27%/+29% QoQ estimate in Q3. We believe the growth is
driven mainly driven by pre-build ahead of further price hikes. The company is seeing

tight supply across major component includes memory, CPU and PCB, but the impact on
deliveries has been limited through August. Since Q226, the company has booked
general server memory on its balance sheet rather than through sales and COGs, which
lifted gross margin in Q2. We factor in lower NRE and mix shifting from general server
now the highest margin with the consigned memory toward ASIC to lower GM/OpM off
the higher Q2 base of 9.3/7.3% to 8.8/6.8% in Q3 and 8.7/6.7% in Q4.

Multiple drivers support another strong year in 2027
We maintain 2027 revenue growth +89% YoY to NT$2,491bn, reflecting strength
across ASICs further ramping and rising liquid penetration toward 30% this generation,
GPUs with Helios racks ramping to 6-7k and general server continuing double digit
volume growth. We keep more conservative on Vera Rubin following limited shipments
on GB300 although should begin shipping in small volumes in Q426 and contributing

~2% of sales in 2027.
Reiterate our BUY with  price target of NT$2,470 (7,350 pre-split)
Following the recent 3-1 stock split, all per-share data below are on a post-split basis. We
reiterate our Buy rating and keep our basic EPS of NT$226.4/NT$174.1 reflecting better
margins, intact ASIC and general server ramps and customer AMD project from Q4 into
2027. Our PT of NT$2,470 is based on same 14x P/E on 2027E EPS also with 3% cash
yield (NT$74 cash dividend). We maintain a positive view reflecting Wiwynn's leverage

to ASIC servers, AMD racks, alongside healthy general server demand.

Forecast excess return 10.0%
Company Description
Spun off from Wistron in March 2012, Wiwynn designs and manufactures cloud
infrastructure equipment for hyperscaler data centres. Prior to 2015, Wiwynn provided
storage servers and traditional servers. In 2015, Wiwynn began to shift its focus to

manufacturing full rack server for hyperscale data centre clients. Its key clients include US
Facebook (50-60% of total revenue), Microsoft (40-50%) and Amazon. Wiwynn is the
second-largest white-box server manufacturer in terms of shipments. It has production sites
in Mexico, the Czech Republic and China.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's
interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of

shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on PE multiple methodology.
Key downside risks for Wiwynn include: 1) a slowdown in the US CSP capex cycle; 2) margin
compression due to intense competition; and 3) unfavourable FX movement.

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.
Views contained in this assessment on a particular stock reflect only the views on those short term factors which are a different timeframe to

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS
Global Research reports; and (ii) where no applicable research report was published that month, the Quantitative Research Review which can
be found at https://neo.ubs.com/quantitative, or contact your UBS sales representative for access to the report or the Quantitative Research
Team on ubs-quant-answers@ubs.com. A consolidated report which contains all responses is also available and again you should contact your

UBS sales representative for details and pricing or the Quantitative Research Team on the email above.
Wiwynn

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Hon Precision (7769.TW)

2
Hon Precision
Valuation
Our target price of NT$11,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 36x applied to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates.
Our PER target of 35x is set at 1-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC testing handler
market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR of 77% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in 2022-

25. Our estimated 77% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average (Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing
equipment peers. As its peers have been trading at  average  36x PER on 2027/28 consensus EPS in the past 1 year, we believe
Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target PER of 36x.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we
are not assigning a High Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of >75%, significantly stronger than the growth outlook

for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker
capacity build for semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI projects; 3) slower-than-expected
capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-expected

development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak
adoption of high-premium cold plate products; and 7) increasing competition from new entrants.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

Document 081: 260921_daiwa_winway

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-21

Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide an update on Winway’s business outlook.

What’s the impact: Marked revenue upside, but a more conservative 
gross margin outlook. We raise our 3Q26 revenue forecast by 9.3% to 
TWD5.0bn (+177.8% YoY) to reflect the stronger-than-expected execution 
of capacity expansion at Renwu Phase 1. Management guided for flat QoQ 
revenue in 4Q26, due to: 1) seasonality; 2) existing capacity being nearly 
fully utilised, with additional capacity scheduled to come online in 1H27; 
and 3) potential revenue recognition delays into 1H27 to accommodate 
customers’ schedules. On profitability, we lower our 3Q26E gross margin 
by 1.8pp, as we expect higher revenue contribution from probe cards and 
incremental depreciation from the new plant to dilute margins. Overall, for 
2026E and 2027E, we raise our revenue by 3.2% and 0.9%, while lowering 
our gross margin by 1.2pp and 1.7pp, respectively.

Probe card share concerns appear overdone. In response to concerns 
over probe card order losses, we believe the expanding TAM will attract 
competitors; the key differentiator is whether suppliers can scale capacity 
to meet customers’ demands. Winway does not expect its probe card share 
at its largest customer to decline and remains confident in maintaining its 
market-leading position. Its Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS) 
probe card supply agreement with Technoprobe (TPRO IM, Not Rated) 
remains in place, which should support its technology competitiveness.

Socket capacity expansion remains on track. Winway doubled its socket 
capacity YoY in 2026 and remains on track to reach monthly pogo-pin 
capacity of 14m units by 1H27, per management. We estimate monthly 
capacity to grow further to 16-18m units by end-2027. Management’s long-
term target is to raise its pogo-pin self-sufficiency ratio to 50-60%, which 
should support gross margin expansion through greater vertical integration.

What we recommend: We slightly raise revenue top-line but lower our 
2026-28E EPS by 1-5% to factor in conservative gross margin 
assumptions. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call, with 12M TP of TWD10,000, 
based on a PER of 50x, applied to our 2027E forward EPS. Our 2026-28E 
EPS CAGR of 100% makes our target PER undemanding, given that the 
resulting PEG ratio is 0.5x. Key downside risks: intensifying competition.

How we differ: Our revised 2026-27E EPS are now roughly in line with 
consensus, reflecting a more prudent gross margin. While market noise 
persists, we see no structural threat to Winway's leading position in sockets 
and probe cards.

21 September 2026

Capacity ramp drives revenue upside

➢ Revenue upside with more prudent gross margin assumptions 
➢ Probe card position intact; socket expansion on track 
➢ Reaffirming Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD10,000

Company profile 
Founded in Kaohsiung in 2001, WinWay has evolved from an optoelectronic test-fixture supplier into a 
leading logic test-interface provider. Its portfolio includes test sockets, MEMS probe cards and contact 
elements. Growth is driven by increasing AI/HPC testing complexity, GPU and ASIC design wins, 
expanding SLT adoption and next-generation HyperSocket solutions.

### Page 2

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Strategy and positioning: Management launched PhotonLink as an architecture-
agnostic integrated optics platform covering CPO, NPO, and chip-to-chip optical
connections. It spans the full optical chain: Generating the light, shaping and
guiding it, and detecting it at the far end. Management discussed that its
customers asked it to supply complete assemblies rather than individual
components as stitching together lasers, fiber and passive optics is difficult. It

argued that no other company holds the full portfolio in-house and that Coherent
can manufacture at datacenter scale. Management expects a mix of adoption
paths, with some customers going straight to CPO and others starting with NPO
and moving to CPO later.
Figure 1. COHR's Portfolio of Optical AI Networking Products
Source: COHR at ECOC
Customers and content: In a full-solution example (combining the ELS, the
assembly to the switch or XPU, and the assembly out to the faceplate),

management said Coherent content could reach up to $15k of content per
switch/processor chip needing 100T of I/O capacity at 200G optical lane. Coherent
counts more than 10 customer engagements each in CPO and NPO and more than
5 in chip-to-chip, with activity going up sharply over the past six months. For CPO,
management said it has an anchor customer with an LTA secured, which it
identified as NVDA. It also has an anchor customer and LTA for NPO, which it did

not name. Separately, management cited two LTAs for ultra-high-power CW lasers
with leading hyperscale or AI datacenter customers, also unnamed.

### Page 3

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Figure 2. PhontonLink Platform
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Timing: CPO for scale-out, tied to the NVIDIA agreement, begins ramping in the
December quarter (calendar Q4 2026). CPO for scale-up follows in the second half
of calendar 2027. NPO ramps in the second half of next calendar year, mainly for
scale-up. Chip-to-chip is further out, around 2029-2030.
Light generation: The ultra-high-power CW laser sits in an external laser source

that holds up to eight lasers, each feeding roughly four links. It starts ramping this
quarter, with significant volume next year, on the 6-inch InP platform in Sherman,
Texas. For NPO, a high-power buried-heterostructure CW laser is already shipping
in millions of units per month for pluggables. VCSEL arrays come in a conventional
version for ribbon fiber and a high-density 2D version that can be flip-chipped
directly to drivers and TIAs with backside lenses. Photodetectors run to 200G

today, with 400G in development.
Figure 3. UHP CW Laser Leader
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026

### Page 4

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Fab footprint: Sherman runs 6-inch InP and GaAs, Sweden runs 6-inch InP . Zurich
runs 6-inch GaAs and is adding 6-inch InP for high-power lasers, while Fremont
runs a 3-inch line. GaAs capacity has grown fourfold since the 6-inch move about
six years ago, with capacity for over one billion VCSEL arrays per year. InP capacity
doubled last year, a quarter ahead of schedule, and management plans to double it

again or more.
Figure 4. COHR Capacity Expansion
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Shape, guide, and receive: Coherent grows garnet in the United States and says it
supplies a significant fraction of the industry's garnet and isolators, with
customers signing LTAs for capacity. The ELS is built on the transceiver line and is
sampling now. The rest of the portfolio covers specialty fiber (polarization-
maintaining fiber production-ready, mode-matched fiber introduced at ECOC,

multi-core fiber), micro-lens arrays, V-grooves, fiber array units, a detachable
connector, circulators, WDM multiplexers, and fiber shuffles.
Silicon photonics and engines: Management discussed Coherent having an in-
house silicon photonics design team, uses foundries for wafers, and does assembly
and test in-house. It ships 200G-per-lane silicon photonics in transceivers today
and showed a 400G link at OFC with a post-deadline paper. It has a 6.4T silicon

photonics NPO module (32 lanes of 200G) and a VCSEL-based NPO with 2D
arrays, which management says achieves about 1.2 pJ/bit including the laser.
Architecture trade-off: Management said NPO is easier to deploy and service but
costs power and signal integrity versus CPO, and that a common NPO socket can
take silicon photonics or VCSELs. It positioned Coherent as an honest advisor
because it can compare all the options. Chip-to-chip is a growing topic, with WDM

and circulators important there.

### Page 5

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
5
Figure 5. COHR CPO Offerings
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Figure 6. COHR NPO Offerings
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Key takeaways from Citi discussion with management:
What is Coherent's competitive advantage in 3.2T, and will it participate?
Management said it has already demonstrated the building blocks for the 3.2T
generation, including 400G-per-lane EMLs and 400G silicon photonics, and
expects to participate just as it enables 1.6T today. Management showed these

demonstrations as early as two years ago.
What is the gross margin path, and what offsets mix pressure? Management
mentioned that the 42%+ target was deliberately open-ended and that it expects
to exceed it. It cautioned against expecting a step change over the next two to
three quarters. Fast transceiver growth is a mix headwind, offset by overhead
absorption, yield gains and factory consolidation. Management expects the 800G-
to-1.6T transition to be margin-accretive, with the cutover forecast around 2028.

### Page 6

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
6
OCS, shipping for two to three quarters, is the first of the new platforms to
generate revenue. OCS, CPO and multi-rail will contribute more to margins as mix
builds.
What are the challenges and economics of the 6-inch InP transition?
Management said it announced 6-inch capability in March 2024 after years of
work with substrate vendors that began before the AI boom. Output is expected to
be roughly 50/50 between 3-inch and 6-inch by the end of the quarter, with all

incremental capacity on 6-inch, reaching about 75% by the end of 2027. A 6-inch
die costs about half as much per yielded die, and each line delivers about four
times the capacity. Yields are better on modern tooling. Management cited a three-
to-four-year lead over competitors and secured substrate supply from five
suppliers across three continents. It also credited its 6-inch GaAs VCSEL line to a
large consumer-electronics customer that demanded the larger wafer size.

Will Coherent keep external InP suppliers? COHR only sources EMLs externally
and that it will keep some external supply for continuity on a critical component.
How does Coherent differ from Chinese laser suppliers? Management argued
that module assembly is easier to replicate but high-speed lasers are not. It said
there are no high-speed EMLs or broadband tunable lasers from China, and that
Chinese CW lasers top out around 70-100 mW. It sees only one InP laser

competitor worth naming and expects new Chinese module entrants to take share
from existing Chinese suppliers rather than from Coherent.
Does NPO vs CPO matter for content, and can low-power lasers serve NPO?
Management said it does not matter much, because the components and dollar
content are similar. Designs are custom and take 18 to 24 months to reach volume,
and management reiterated scale-up production revenue in the second half of next
year. Management rejected the view that NPO can use low-power lasers, arguing

that coupling several weak lasers loses light and defeats the power-efficiency
purpose.
What is multi-rail, and how is it progressing? Multi-rail addresses scale-across
links between data centers. Management said it delivers four times the capacity in
the same footprint at roughly 30% lower power per lane, enabled by a multi-chip
uncooled pump that it says has no comparable competitor. Small shipments are
underway and production shipments begin in the first half of next calendar year. It

sells at the component, module, line-card and system level, including white-label
to OEMs.

### Page 7

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
7
Coherent Corp
Company description
COHR is a vertically integrated manufacturing company that develops
engineered materials and opto-electronic components for industrial
materials processing, communications, aerospace and defense, consumer
electronics, semiconductor capital equipment, life sciences and automotive
end markets. COHR produces a wide variety of application-specific photonic
and electronic materials and components, and deploys them in various forms,

including integration with advanced software. COHR's customer base
includes original equipment manufacturers, laser end users, system
integrators of high-power lasers, manufacturers of equipment and devices for
industrial, optical communications, consumer electronics, security and
monitoring applications, U.S. government prime contractors, and various U.S.
government agencies. COHR is a headquartered in Pennsylvania.
Investment strategy
We are Buy-rated on COHR. We believe that the stock's multiple will benefit

from the AI appeal while its fundamentals should expand on new
management's strong execution and focus on profitability.
Valuation
We value COHR at $420 or 30x forward P/E applied to FY2028 EPS of ~$16
discounted back. Our COHR valuation is nevertheless at a 10% discount to
the average forward P/E of AI optics peers to reflect a lower AI sales mix and
3-year EPS CAGR vs peers such as LITE.
Risks
Downside risks to the achievement of our target price include: 1) increased

competition on AI and SiC leads to limited market share growth and limited
revenue upside, 2) inability to achieve synergies and margin targets
established post acquisition of Finisar, and 3) prolonged economic recession
leads to slower 5G build-out and lower consumer demand driving revenue of
COHR down.
Upside risks to the achievement of our target price include: 1) gaining share in
AI and SiC market through new product offerings and first to market status

on larger wafer sizes, 2) quicker and stronger adoption of AI workloads,
electric and autonomous vehicles, and 3) quicker return of margins to pre-
Finisar acquisition levels.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

Asus (2357.TW): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix 
upgrade; Aug beat; Neutral
27 September 2026 | 10:33AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep, leading 
3Q26 revenues to grow at 8% QoQ, while we expect sequential revenues decline in 
4Q26 at 7%, considering the PC consumption pull in under the high memory costs 
along with AI servers model transition. We remain positive on the company’s 
expansion toward AI servers and expect the upcoming new models carrying higher 
ASP to further drive its growth in late 2026 / early 2027. We continue to expect 
double digit revenues YoY in 3Q26 / 4Q26E at 39% / 27% YoY driven by PC product 
mix upgrade and AI server racks ramp up. The company retains strong commitments 
on capacity expansion and diversification, with issuance of US$0.5bn of convertible 
bonds and US$1bn of exchangeable bonds, supporting the expansion. Maintain 
Neutral on fair valuation.

Earnings revision: We factor in ASUS’s Aug revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
incomes by 4% / 7% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues. 2026-28E revenues are raised 
by 4% / 4% / 4%, mainly on robust AI server growth momentum, and PC product mix 
upgrade along with rising selling price under the high memory costs.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,115. Our target 
price is based on 14.8x 2027E P/E, which is derived from brand makers’ average ratio of 
2027E P/E to 2028E NI YoY and OPM. We see EPS growth and OPM as major factors for 
the stock’s share performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 24 Sep 2026 close.

Compal (2324.TW): AI servers to fuel 4Q26E/1Q27E growth; Aug miss; 
Neutral
27 September 2026 | 6:06PM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, 
considering the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, 
and PC product mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect 
Compal’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 1% / 16% QoQ, riding on AI server racks 
ramp up and the company’s expansion from motherboard (L6) to system (L10 / L11). 
We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, supported by global 
capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, Vietnam and China, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, for which 
demand could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain 
Neutral on fair valuation.

Compal’s Aug revenue was up 15% YoY / down 17% MoM to NT$67bn, or 20% lower 
than our estimate, reflecting PC shipments decline. Aug PC shipments was down 22% 
MoM to 1.4m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid the rising 
memory cost and weak consumer electronics demand.

Earnings revision: Factoring in Compal’s Aug revenue, we cut our 2026-28E net

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$46.1. Our target 
price is based on 14.7x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 24 Sep 2026 close.

Idea
Current debate: Can MediaTek meet Street 2028 forecasts given Intel EMIB-T
supply issue? Intel's EMIB-T substrate supply has been a potential bottleneck for
future TPU v9 ("Humufish") shipments in 2028, making some investors reluctant to
own MediaTek. The issue stems from a supply-demand mismatch. We forecast 3mn
TPU v9 units, as demand from Google (covered by Brian Nowak) remains strong.
However, as discussed in our AI Supply Chain Tracker, we expect only 2mn EMIB-T

substrates, i.e. 40–45k wafer-equivalent EMIB-T capacity in 2028. This aligns with
Digitimes' report that Google is considering CoWoS as a backup for its 2027 TPU on
lagging EMIB-T substrate yields.  For TSMC, supporting CoWoS could also help
regain market share from EMIB-T. Our checks indicate that both MediaTek and
TSMC are engaging to meet customer (Google) demand.
TSMC's willingness to support production increases confidence in our aggressive

2028 EPS forecast: Assuming TSMC CoWoS-L can support 0.5mn TPU v9 units and
EMIB-T can support 2.5mn units, our 3mn-unit target for 2028 appears highly
achievable, with a higher ASP from the CoWoS version. CoWoS-L carries a
structurally higher cost, and the RDL (redistribution layer) interposer connection
requires tape-out of three additional photomask layers. However, our checks also
suggest that Google is willing to pay these incremental costs to secure dual

sourcing, which should help MediaTek sustain its gross margin.
New debate: Who could be the second CSP customer? Investors have asked
whether MediaTek could win Tesla's AI6 edge AI chip program. We believe AI6 is
better suited to GUC, given its 10%–25% margin profile. Considering the higher tech
content of MediaTek's design services, incl. IP + design capabilities, we believe
MediaTek targets a 30%–35% margin profile. Instead, we see a growing probability

that MediaTek wins a cloud AI ASIC project from an AI-focused CSP and/or AI lab
customer through its  Nvidia / NVLink Fusion partnership. In previous notes, we
highlighted interest from both SpaceX and an AI lab customer. The customer's
decision could come in 4Q26 and serve as a significant catalyst for MediaTek. With
TPU upside and a potential new project, we expect MediaTek AI ASIC-related
revenue of US$13.5bn and US$54bn in 2027 and 2028, resp., representing c.38% and

72% of total revenue. Reiterate Top Pick.

### Page 2

Idea
2
TSMC CoWoS capacity support eases concerns regarding
MediaTek 2028  TPU production
Smartphone market still lukewarm into 2027, but can serve as a long-term option
value: At our recent China Best Conference, meetings involving China smartphone OEM
and NAND module makers appeared to indicate China's smartphone demand may not
recover in 2027, as mobile DRAM prices may not be falling anytime soon. We expect
MediaTek’s 3Q26 revenue to be ahead of guidance because of customer pull-in to avoid

the SoC price hike rather than due to demand improvement.
That said, once AI smartphones become a killer application, MediaTek should rank among
the best-positioned semiconductor companies to provide the edge computing capabilities
required in handsets. We also believe 2028 should be a strong year for MediaTek's
smartphone SoC business.
1. Google TPU supply chain update
TSMC CoWoS may  support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm (Humufish) MediaTek TPU
in 2028: In our earlier report, MediaTek: 2Q26 results preview: Smartphone weak, but

Cloud AI taking off; OW (26 Jul 2026), we noted that  MediaTek may secure a portion of
CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPU to ensure minimum volume production. But, we believe it
will use EMIB-T from Intel (covered by Joseph Moore)  as the primary solution to reduce
costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution proceeds well. Our recent industry checks
suggest TSMC CoWoS would continue to support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm
(Humufish) MediaTek TPU in 2028. We now assume around 500k units of 2nm TPU going

through CoWoS production as a second source. The 3nm TPU in 2028 of around 2mn
units is higher than our initial expectation of 1mn units. We therefore raise our MediaTek
2028 TPU revenue assumption to US$51.5bn.
2nm TPU TAM is supported by different SKU: At our AI Summit, MediaTek management
stated that its design service is capable of both training and inference design. Recently,
investors have been asking whether MediaTek is involved in the design of 2nm TriggerFish

TPU, intended for AI inference. Our supply chain checks suggest this could be true. The
TriggerFish chip has more features (I/O die upgrade, one more simulation die, and change
of HBM controller to HBM4e), so we think MediaTek’s 2nm TPU blended ASP could go
higher. We estimate ASP at US$12-20k (we now model US$13k). But again, the EMIB-T
substrate yield should be the key risk to volume.
CoWoS constraint eased, but ABF substrate supply still the gating factor for TPU bull

case: In our recent AI supply chain tracker, we noted that MediaTek’s 180k CoWoS-S
booking implies ~3.6mn units of TPU 8t (ZebraFish) in 2027, above our original 3mn
shipment assumption. However, we continue to bake in some conservatism as we had
assumed ABF substrate shortages. So,  we still forecast 3mn units in 2027.

2. Who could be the second and third customer for MediaTek
in AI ASICs?
NVLink Fusion partnership should be the key catalyst for MediaTek to win new
projects:
Through the NVLink Fusion platform, MediaTek's XPU customers can leverage NVIDIA's
scale-up and scale-out networking technologies to build AI server systems.
In our report, MediaTek: NVIDIA and Alphabet Invest in MediaTek’s CB for Extensive
Partnership in ASIC; Stay OW, Top Pick (31 Aug 2026), we shared that MediaTek had

maintained its 2027 ASIC TAM and market share guidance despite the broader partnership,
noting that commercialization would take time. However, we believe the partnership
materially increases MediaTek's chances of winning a second CSP customer.
Overall, we see upside to our 2028 ASIC revenue assumptions, driven by: 1) a higher
probability of winning a second or even third CSP customer; 2) greater revenue from
material preparation; and 3) a strengthened partnership with Alphabet on TPU programs.

In today's research, we incorporate potential revenue contributions from new customers.
Exhibit 2: NVLink Fusion to support connection with custom ASIC
Source: Nvidia
MediaTek's second CSP customer could begin contributing revenue from subsystem
builds in 2028: We believe the subsystem would include procurement of key components
such as HBM and ABF substrates, as well as wafer foundry capacity prepayments.

Our checks suggest MediaTek may target subsystem builds for a second CSP customer
rather than supplying the XPU itself. We now expect an initial revenue contribution in
2028, based on approximately 100k subsystem units at an ASP of US$25k.
We therefore assume a US$2.5bn revenue contribution in 2028, based on 100k units and
an ASP of US$25k, including HBM chip consignment, known-good dies (KGDs) for
compute chips, and chiplets.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value creation for customers across

both silicon and packaging design. As a result, we expect ASICs to remain accretive to
operating margins. At the gross margin level, management indicated that margins vary by
project, potentially reflecting support for full-rack systems. We model 2nm TPU gross
margins at c.35% (vs. c.40% for 3nm TPUs) given the larger revenue scale. We continue to
expect operating margins of ~20–25%, above the smartphone business unit's 15–20%.

3. Why TSMC is motivated  to support MediaTek and Google?
TSMC's CoWoS-L solution carries a structurally higher cost due to its larger reticle size, as
we discussed in The Foundry Floorplan: TSMC preview update; CoWoS vs. EMIB
competition (14 Apr 2026). We believe TSMC also wants to address growing competition
from Intel's EMIB-T, which we estimate will reach 40-45k wafer equivalent capacity 2028,

implying a 10-15% share of the 2.5D packaging market.
For MediaTek to adopt TSMC's CoWoS-L as a second source alongside EMIB-T, the RDL
(redistribution layer) connection to the interposer requires tape-out of three additional
photomask layers. However, our checks suggest that Google is willing to absorb the
incremental costs associated with dual sourcing, which should allow MediaTek to sustain
its gross margin.

produce close to 2.5mn units of Humufish from late 2027-2028, by assuming 4-5 chips per
wafer of EMIB-T, while TSMC CoWoS could support around 500k units.
We see current average EMIB-M capacity being 110kwpm, which would slightly decrease

to 95kwpm in 2027 as Intel is converting some capacities toward EMIB-T.
Exhibit 6: We expect Intel's EMIB-T capacity to reach 20kwpm
in 2027 and 45kwpm in 2028; EMIB-M capacity is currently at
110kwpm, which would be down to 95kwpm due to conversion

8
Raise price target from NT$5,588 to NT$6,188
We continue to derive our price target (our base-case scenario value) from a residual
income model. The price target change mostly reflects our earnings estimate changes. All
of  our key valuation assumptions remain unchanged: cost of equity at 9.2% (beta of 1.2,
risk-free rate of 2%, and risk premium of 6%), intermediate growth rate of 12%, and
terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Our bull- and bear-case scenario values rise from NT$7,675 to NT$8,500 and  NT$2,845 to

NT$3,150, respectively.

### Page 9

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 9
Relative valuation
We view the P/E as relatively attractive – even after the share price rally – given strong AI
demand. Although MediaTek faces smartphone headwinds in 2026, we believe it is time to
look beyond 2026. We think this margin pressure is fully priced in, while TPU upside is
not. We believe the stock should trade more than +1SD above its historical average,
supported by strong TPU demand. Our  price target of NT$6,188 implies 42x  2027 EPS, on

our estimates.

MS Rating
▪ We expect MediaTek's gross margin to
decline in both 2026 and 2027 as a result of
smartphone headwinds and TPU margin
dilution, but strong demand from TPU

should drive earnings growth.
▪ Overall AI ASIC demand looks very strong,
especially from Google's TPU, which should
beneﬁt MediaTek in the coming years.
▪ The AI smartphone replacement cycle is
the key wild card. AI
smartphone replacement could be too mild
to pass through additional costs in Chinese
smartphones.
▪ Risk-reward is attractive at the current
level given strong TPU demand. We believe
the stock could re-rate to 42x 2027e P/E,
more than double its 18x historical average

since 2006.

58x 2027e EPS
MediaTek receives US licenses for shipments
to all Chinese smartphone brands. Emerging
market demand recovers to trend-line
growth. MediaTek reaches 90% of an
enlarged addressable market for 5G Android

in premium smartphone models. Blended
ASP rises in 2027. Better business
development in IoT, autos, enterprise

ASIC business, especially on Google TPU
demand.
NT$6,188.00
42x 2027e EPS
MediaTek maintains around 35-40% overall
share in the Chinese smartphone market,
with a share of 30-40% in 5G SoC for 2025
(ex-Apple). Besides smartphones, AI ASIC
should be the largest focus for the company

now. We expect a >50% revenue CAGR
during 2025-28. Non-smartphone business
development stays on track, including IoT,
autos, enterprise networking chips, edge AI
projects and AI ASIC business.
NT$3,150.00
21x 2027e EPS
Competitive pressures are worse than

which hurts gross margin. Emerging market
smartphone growth stalls. Chinese
smartphone chipset vendors show a
breakthrough in 5G technologies or foundry
supply and start to take more market share

in mid-range 5G smartphone SoCs. Slow
new business development in ASIC, IoT and
automotive, edge AI projects and AI ASIC
business.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

### Page 2

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•MediaTek’s advanced-node and supply chain capabilities are underestimated — We believe MediaTek’s Google
TPU order can scale materially into 2028 and beyond despite market concerns regarding possible project delays. The
TPU V8 chip is already in production, and we believe it is on track for US$2bn revenue in 2026. MediaTek has also
consistently been a top 5 customer at TSMC for the most advanced node with big volumes for several years. They

have multiple N2 projects and early work extending toward A14. This matters because leading-edge ASIC execution
is not only about SerDes, on which MediaTek is also catching up with global peers; the RTL design would require
advanced node design and technology co-optimization, physical implementation, foundry capacity management,
package and substrate coordination, testing, and high-volume ramp. Under Google’s COT/semi-custom model, this
capability should become even more relevant as MediaTek provides design, integration, and manufacturing

execution.
•EMIB-T risk for TPU v9, yet CoWoS/CoPoS for v10 likely to succeed — While investors are also skeptical about
MediaTek’s SerDes technology, we note that its 224G SerDes is in production and that 336G and 448G SerDes is
actively under development. MediaTek is also developing die-to-die, which has already been silicon-validated on
TSMC N3P and N2P. For advanced packaging, we would nevertheless not dismiss EMIB-T yield risk for TPU v9 and

large-scale chiplet integration. Yet we believe there is strong chance that MediaTek will be working on
CoWoS/CoPoS for TPU v10, which is aiming for 2029 production. And its higher value for v9 and long lift v8 should
be more than enough to compensate for the potential shipment downside into 2028.
•Growing with expanding TPU opportunity + new customer engagement — There have been reports that Marvell,
AMD, and Samsung Foundry are potential alternatives for high-end HBM4E inference TPU and future TPU V10

designs. In our view, Google’s expanding TPU TAM should support multiple suppliers and TPU V10 is more likely a
2029–2030 generation. We believe MediaTek and TSMC will continue to be the main source. Given MediaTek’s tight
relationship with Nvidia and the NVLink Fusion ecosystem, we believe it has other hyperscale opportunities such as
Microsoft and xAI.
•Maintaining Buy; more than doubling AI revenue into 2028 from 2027 — In our view, Mediatek is emerging as a

credible hyperscale ASIC partner with materially stronger advanced-node, chiplet, SerDes, and supply-chain
capabilities than the bear case implies. EMIB-T and first-generation large-chiplet execution remain the key risks, but
MediaTek’s rapid learning curve, manufacturing scale, cost discipline, and Google’s capacity-sharing model provide
meaningful mechanisms to mitigate them. We estimate MediaTek’s TPU shipment to be at 4m units for 2027 and
2028, but 2028 revenue to double compared to 2027 due to higher ASP on upgraded SerDes (336G) and N2

advanced node top die design effort.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our views on Delta after c.4 months of share price 
weakness, driven by investor concerns on HVDC delays, and a slowdown 
in power consumption trend amid deceleration in Nvidia’s GPU shipment 
growth YoY. We remain positive on Delta over 2026-28E, as rising AI ASIC 
server trend should drive a meaningful increase in DC-DC power module 
ASPs (vs. Nvidia’s discrete form factor). We expect higher HVDC adoption 
from 2028E, driven by IT rack and power supply architecture changes.

What’s the impact: Component shortages are the key near-term 
bottleneck. We attribute the recent share-price weakness to slower-than-
expected revenue momentum in both 2Q26, due to vendor-sanction issue 
at Delta Thailand, and 3Q26E, due to a worsening shortage of power 
semiconductors. We believe the 2Q26 revenue shortfall was a one-off 
event, though 3Q26E growth is likely to remain constrained by the power-
semi shortage. While supply capacity should improve, the supply-demand 
gap will likely persist for some time. That said, end-demand should remain 
sound. Given the continued improvement in revenue momentum, we still 
expect operating margin to reach a record-high of 18.3% in 3Q26.

Long-term growth drivers. We see Delta as a key beneficiary of the AI 
ASIC server trend. We were previously concerned that rising AI ASIC 
adoption could hurt Delta, as ASICs have lower thermal design power 
(TDP) than Nvidia GPUs, but now believe that Delta will benefit from rising 
content value of DC-DC power modules, as hyperscalers are more willing 
to spend on DC-DC modules (vs. discrete) despite higher initial cost given 
their superior energy efficiency. We forecast DC-DC power module revenue 
to reach c.TWD60bn in 2026E (vs. TWD9bn in 2025 and our prior estimate 
of c.TWD20bn for 2026E). This figure could increase further with rising 
ASIC usage in 2027. We also believe HVDC adoption rate could reach at 
least 50% in 2028E (vs. our prior expectation of 30%), as power supply 
architecture is becoming increasingly bulky (to accommodate aluminium 
capacitors inside power supply amid the super capacitor shortage). Limited 
space in IT racks would force customers to adopt more power racks and 
likely equip them with high voltage versions to improve efficiency.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 2-15% on higher 
HVDC contribution. We lift our 12M TP to TWD3,088 (from TWD2,988) 
based on a lower target PER of 49x (from 55x) to reflect market sentiment, 
(at the high-end of its past-3-year range of 11-50x) applied to our 1-year-
forward EPS. Key downside risk: lower-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2028E earnings is 21% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish assumption for HVDC adoption.

30 September 2026

Key beneficiary of the AI ASIC server trend

➢ Better-than-expected DC-DC AI server contribution from 2026E 
➢ HVDC adoption rate should be higher-than-expected in 2028E 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating and raising 12M TP to TWD3,088

Company profile 
Founded in 1971, Delta Electronics has been the global leader in switching power supply solutions since 
2002 and DC brushless fans since 2006. Its business covers 3 key areas: power electronics (eg, power 
supplies, thermal management products and passive components), automation (eg, controllers, robot 
arms and building management systems), and infrastructure (eg, datacentre infrastructure, networking 
and EV charging).

Update Date: 30 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Delta's board has 7 non-independent and 5 independent directors with a term of three years.
The total number of directors and ratio of independent directors appear higher than most other
listed companies. Besides, according to Delta's CSR report, enhancing the board's functions is
one of the key goals for the company to achieve better corporate governance; also, the board
members' diversity and professionalism will be taken into consideration to ensure a good board
quality. Moreover, the company evaluates all the directors in the board using internal regulations
and an external independent institution each year and at least once every three years,
respectively. We believe Delta has implemented relatively more enhanced measure than most
listed firms to improve its board quality.
Capital management 2
During 2016-19, Delta's payout ratio was stable in the range of c.70%, but fell to 56% in 2020
and 53% in 2021. For 2020, it was mainly due to a sizeable non-cash re-valuation gain after the
acquisition of Delta Thailand (DET), while the company maintained more cash on hand to deal
with the uncertainties caused by COVID-19. However, the DPS growth from 2016 to 2021 was
limited, as Delta struggled with business diversification into new end-markets (eg, smartphone,
NEV). We believe that Delta's effort started to bear fruit in recent years after its NEV business
has gradually picked up. Overall, we see a stable dividend policy from Delta and expect its DPS
to continue to grow YoY in the future given that they declared a TWD11.6 DPS for 2025 which
equals a 50% payout ratio.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue from related parties stood at 12% of total 2025 revenue and purchases from related
parties were at 11%. We see limited risk from related party transactions.
S Supply chain
management 1
Delta focuses on: 1) localised management, 2) supplier certification and risk management, and
3) supplier sustainability engagement as key initiatives under the scope of supply chain
management. In 2019, 144 new suppliers joined Delta's supply chain system. All of them passed
environmental and social evaluations, signed the declaration of "non-use of conflict materials",
and complied with Responsible Business Alliance's (BRA) Code of Conduct. Also, these vendors
had to implement and abide by Delta's supply chain CSR policy and code of conduct. To lower
ESG risks, Delta carries out RBA audits and monitors high-risk critical suppliers each year. The
suppliers that fail these audits have to provide detailed improvement plans in response to
identified issues. Besides, Delta has surveyed a total of 263 suppliers with potential risk and 63
suppliers with high risk of the total 1,331 suppliers, based on its CSR policy and code of conduct.
If these suppliers are flagged as high-risk and no concrete improvements are made, Delta may
consider terminating cooperation and transactions with them to improve the company's
engagement on supplier sustainability. Overall, we think Delta has a solid plan to improve its
supply chain management with specific KPIs, including: 1) achieving supplier improvement rate
of 80% in 2025, 2) reducing GHG emissions rate of critical suppliers by 10% in 2025, and 3)
improving water saving rate of critical suppliers by 12% in 2025.
S Data security 2
Delta's Information Security team created Delta's Information Security Policy in November 2013
and started providing educational training to global employees in 2014. Delta gradually
introduced the Information Security Management System (ISMS) in 2016 and obtained the ISO
27001 international certification in 2018. According to ISO27001, Delta has to conduct at least
one internal audit on information security and appoint an independent institution to conduct audit
externally. According to Delta, no major deficiencies have been found in recent years.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

2% MoM and 65% YoY in August 2026 (vs. -5% MoM/+50% YoY in July 2026), to
¥198bn.

¥52bn

¥146bn
• By region:
° China: -2% MoM/+62% YoY, to ¥47bn
° Europe: -12% MoM/+44% YoY, to ¥20bn
° North America: +24% MoM/+72% YoY, to ¥50bn
Our view:
• We think the solid YoY growth for overseas orders continues to point to an
upcycle scenario, despite some seasonal volatility  with MoM growth in some
regions,
• On top of overall segment recovery within automation:
° Airtac (1590.TW, OW) should benefit from its own share gains and
incremental contributions from linear motion components. Valuation

appears undemanding at 17x 2027e P/E (vs. 25x average since 2020).
° Hiwin (2049.TW, OW) should show margin expansion in the coming
quarters, supported by higher utilization and price hikes. We see
potential share price upside as the stock now trades at 29x 2027e P/E
(vs. peak cycle valuation of 35-40x).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

本次報告：論壇

欣興 (3037 TT) 
ABF 漲幅優於預期，獲利再度上修

永豐觀點 
3Q26 欣興 ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點，中線T-glass 玻纖布於 3Q27 前
供給吃緊，長線 ABF 供不應求至 2030 年，載板報價可望逐季推升 5-15%，獲利
持續高速成長，近期股價下跌反應檢調搜索利空，評價已修正至合理區間。 
 投資評價與建議 
欣興受惠 ABF 帶動產品組合轉佳，長線而言 ABF 於 2030 年前供給吃緊，載板報
價漲勢由成本推升改為需求拉動，3Q26 漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點，且本波循環
公司通吃 CoWoS 及 EMIB-T 訂單，客戶遍及 GPU、ASIC 及 CPU 訂單，技術能
力僅次於龍頭廠 Ibiden， 研究部再度上調2026-2028 年稅後 EPS 至 23.57、42.71
及 65.08 元，投資建議買進，考量檢調風波對評價承壓，目標價維持 1575 元
(24X2028 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
欣興企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2023 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

欣興為國內 ABF 龍頭廠商：欣興為國內專業 IC 載板製造商，全球市佔率 18%僅
次於 Ibiden 以及 Samsung，2Q26 產品組合為 ABF(52%)、BT(9%)、HDI(27%)、
PCB(9%)、FPC(2%)及其它(1%)，其中 IC 載板毛利率優於公司平均水準。下游客
戶包含 Nokia、APPLE、Nvidia 以及 HTC，主要競爭對手為 Ibiden、Samsung、
Shinko、南電以及景碩。 
3Q26 營收達成率 69.6%， 暗示ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 ：欣興 8 月營收來到 177.46
億元(+9.2%MoM，+56.3%YoY)，除延續自 3 月以來月月創高紀錄外值得留意的
是月增及年增幅度亦同步擴大，整體 3Q26 營收達成率 69.6%亦優於原預期，暗
示 ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點以上，研究部上調3Q26 營收至 525.83 億
元(+22.6%QoQ，+54.7%YoY)， 毛利率25.8%， 稅後淨利93.36 億元(-28.8%QoQ，
+325.4%YoY)，稅後 EPS 5.68 元(原估 5.26 元)。 
CPU 生命週期展開使 ABF 需求更具延續性：過去 ABF 需求主要聚焦 AI Server 需
求，研究部原預估 2024-2025 年 ABF 供過於求 2%，2026 年亦難見反轉。受惠
AI Server 於 GPU 及 ASIC 需求帶動，另一方面 general server 回溫有望使 ABF
需求由高階產品延續至中階產品，Ibiden 預估相關高階 ABF 需求高速成長，加計
Production load 換算 2028 年面積需求較 2024 年增加 10.7 倍 ， 相較AI/ASIC 產
能於 2028 年擴充 1.8 倍供給仍低於需求。 
檢調搜索風波影響營運有限：日前欣興合江廠遭檢調搜索，股價亦應聲下跌，研究
部認為欣興合江廠位於中壢，主要生產 PCB 產品 2Q26 營收占比 9%，雖然後續
可能因產地標示不明影響 PCB 客戶關係 ， 惟與目前主力營運ABF 載板以及 HDI 並
無關聯。ABF 在 AI Server 需求推動下 2030 年以前供不應求，目前高階 GPU、
ASIC 產品僅 Ibiden(4062 JP)及欣興(3037 TT)具備相關供應能力，自研 CPU 則有
景碩(3189 TT) 緊追在後，考量產品認證需時 6-8 個月、關鍵壓合機台 lead time
亦 1-1.5 年，短期轉單不易。 
ABF 報價優於預期，上調 2026-2028 年營運：欣興 2026 年資本支出 537 億元，
其中 85%用於 ABF 除搭配客戶陸續啟動光復及楊梅 2 廠擴產，楊梅 3 廠可望於
4Q26-1Q27 動土，以土建時程 1.5-2 年推算 2029 年可望加入營運行列，未來 2
年資本支出亦可望維持 400 億以上水準。研究部考量 ABF 整體供給吃緊態勢延續
以及公司通吃 CoWoS 及 EMIB-T 產品，先進封裝客戶群布局廣泛，研究部上調

OnielChen@yuanta.com

CPU 需求強勁、營收進度超前，調高 26 年營收成長至 8 成 
展望 2H26 延續 CPU 需求強勁、MSFT Maia 200/300 及中國車用專案放量，
加上台積更多產能的支持，營收將優於 1H26 且呈季季高走勢，然 7、8 月
營收達成進度超前，因而預估 3Q26 營收季增>2 成及 26 全年營收年增近 8
成。元大預估 3Q26 營收季增 23%，毛利率略降，推估 3Q26 EPS13.76 元/
季增 19%。4Q26 為傳統入帳高峰及延續 CPU 動能，中國車用專案量產等，
營收為全年最高，毛利率有機會持平至略佳，推估 EPS16.96 元/季增 23%。 
CSP 朝 COT 模式，帶動更多 IC 設計服務委外的商機 
CSP 由傳統晶片廠統包朝向 COT(Customer Owned Tooling) 分工模式，轉
由 CSP 主 導 晶 片 架 構 與 前 端 設 計 流 程 ， 將 資 源 集 中 於 最 具 價 值 的
Architecture/RTL 前端設計，將實體設計、先進封裝、chiplet 後端設計服務
分拆外包，基於晶片複雜度提升及開發成本墊高，藉由分工達分散供應鏈風
險，對傳統 ASIC 廠商造成價值鏈瓜分，對 backend 委外需求增加反而有利
於純 設 計 服務 廠 商 。創 意布局 包括 HBM2/3/3E/4/4E 、GLink 相關 IP、
CoWoS/SOIC/DoD 解決方案，自有 IP 具客製化彈性，在 TSMC APT 生態
系的重要提升，承接底層設計機會大增，為 COT 模式下的主要受惠者之一。  
車用 AI 專案預計 2Q27~3Q27 量產，進入更強勁的成長週期 
展望 2026 年營收成長來自 CSP N3 CPU ，估計營收佔比>5 成。AI 擴大至
推論應用，CPU 需求強勁及產能獲得支援，使得 26~27 年展望較先前樂觀，
並且延續 2H27~2028 年主要成長來自車用客戶 AI 專案放量，預估 2027~28
年 AI 營收佔比>8 成。而 Google、Meta 等潛在效益落在 29 年。元大最新預
估 2026/27/28 EPS 54.6/113.1/163.47 元，分別上修 7%/12%/11%，係因
上修 CPU 出貨量及 Maia 營收貢獻、毛利率略降 0.5 個百分點，北美車用分
別估計 27、28 年營收佔比 28%、41%，費用假設略降。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
2H26 CPU 需求強勁、MSFT Maia 系列及中國車用專案放量，加上台
積產能支持等，營運展望樂觀。  
⧫  
隨著 AI 擴大至推論/代理型應用，帶動 CPU 需求爆發及營運上修，延
續至 27~28 年接續美系車用專案量產，29 年來自 CSP 轉向 COT 效益
推升營運達另一高峰。 
⧫  
預估 2026/27/28 年 EPS 5 5/113/163 元，上修 7%/12%/11%，維持買
進，目標本益比升至 50 倍、2028 年 EPS，推得目標價 8200 元。基
於 Agentic AI、COT 趨勢有利公司營運發展，評價面亦具上修空間。

Agentic AI  帶動 CPU 價值重估、公司營運上修 
隨 AI 大型模型成熟漸漸由訓練轉向 Inference/agentic AI ，預期全球 Token 需求每三個月翻倍，其
中 ASIC 可針對客戶需求打造客製化晶片，算力使用效率較 GPU 更貼近客戶需求、較佳的性價比。
全球 AI ASIC 市場 25~28 年 CAGR 達 35~40%，估計 ASIC 滲透率將突破 3 成。Agentic AI 在於
主動任務執行，相較於過往 LLM 矩陣運算，強調 CPU/GPU/ASIC 的協同運算需求，帶動 CPU 搭
配比例提升、創意營運上修空間。 
CSP 朝 COT 模式，帶動更多 IC 設計服務委外的商機 
CSP 由傳統晶片廠統包朝向 COT(Customer Owned Tooling) 分工模式，轉由 CSP 主導晶片架構與
前端設計流程，將資源集中於最具價值的 Architecture/RTL 前端設計，將實體設計、先進封裝、
chiplet 後端設計服務分拆外包，基於晶片複雜度提升及開發成本墊高，藉由分工達分散供應鏈風險，
對傳統 ASIC 廠商造成價值鏈瓜分，對 backend 委外需求增加反而有利於純設計服務廠商。創意布
局包括 HBM2/3/3E/4/4E、GLink 相關 IP、CoWoS/SOIC/DoD 解決方案，自有 IP 具客製化彈性，
在 TSMC APT 生態系的重要提升，承接底層設計機會大增，為 COT 模式下的主要受惠者之一。 
車用 AI 專案預計 2Q27~3Q27 量產，進入更強勁的成長週期 
展望 2026 年營收成長來自 CSP N3 CPU ，估計營收佔比>5 成。AI 擴大至推論/代理型應用，CPU
需求強勁及產能獲得台積支援，使得公司 26~27 年展望較先前樂觀，並且延續 2H27~2028 年主要
成長來自車用客戶 AI 專案放量，預估 2027~28 年 AI 營收佔比>8 成。另外，CSP 轉向 COT 模式，
包括 Google、Meta 等潛在效益落在 29 年。元大最新預估 2026/27/28 EPS 54.6/113.1/163.47 元，
分別上修 7%/12%/11%，係因上修 CPU 出貨量及 Maia200/300 營收貢獻、毛利率略降 0.5 個百分
點，北美車用分別估計 27、28 年營收佔比 28%、41%，費用假設略降。

Agentic AI 將帶動 CPU 需求大幅提升 
Agentic AI 不再只是單次 prompt-response，需要反覆進行任務規劃、推理、檢查與修正。相較傳
統生成式 AI，工作流程更長、步驟更多，對系統層級調度能力要求大幅提升。 CPU 在 Agentic AI 
中主要負責 orchestration、資料前後處理，以及外部工具與資料源調用。包含  Web、Database、
API 等任務管理，協助 GPU / ASIC 專注於模型運算。隨 agent 工作流變長，CPU 與 GPU 的協同
效率將影響整體延遲、吞吐量，成為未來 AI server 架構升級重點。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 9 月 21 日 Global Unichip 第4 頁，共 14頁  
圖 5：Agentic AI 的執行流程需要反覆的調用 CPU 與 GPU 的運算資源

資料來源：Semianalysis、元大投顧

解決推論延遲需提高 CPU 用量並優化演算法 
代理式 AI 涉及大量任務分解、環境切換、API 互動與外部工具調用，相關 orchestration latency 可
占總延遲約 50%-90%。搜尋網頁、分析內容、執行  Python 腳本與查詢資料庫等非模型推論任務，
多由 CPU 負責執行，因此增加 CPU 資源可直接緩解瓶頸。透過 COMB (CPU 感知微批次處理) 與 
MAS 可優化 CPU-GPU 協同演算法：COMB 將大批次任務拆分，使 CPU 與 GPU 可更同步執行，
研究顯示可提升約 1.8 倍運算效率。MAS 則依任務屬性區分 CPU-heavy 與 GPU-heavy workload，
建立彈性資源調度，避免任務壟斷整體運算資源。

圖 6：當模型執行更複雜任務需要更多 CPU

資料來源：Semianalysis、元大投顧

不同的代理 AI 場景，CPU:GPU 之比例約落在 0.8-1.4:1 
代理式 AI 的 CPU 需求取決於工作流中工具調用與資料處理比例。以網頁資料蒐集為例， DOM 
parsing、摘要生成、網路 I/O 與資料整理多由 CPU 負責，CPU 延遲占比較高。因此若應用場景偏
向資料蒐集、網頁解析與外部工具互動， CPU:GPU 最適比例將提高至約  1.4:1，以降低整體推論
瓶頸。 
Excel 自動化、 Python / Bash 腳本執行與自動生成簡報，同樣需要大量  CPU 參與。相關任務
CPU 延遲占比約 25%-65%。因此，自動化與文件生成的 agent workflow，也可將 CPU:GPU 比例
提高至約 0.8:1，以改善整體任務效率。

圖 7：Agentic AI 應用場景
